passthread_acceptanceblocking

The complete thread is a strong semantic match. All eight Heartbreakers receive distinct, location-specific opening beats: Chloe and Liam are sincere and vulnerable, Jordan is an anxious joker, Maya is openly flirty, Marcus is confidently bromantic, Nia is observant and warm, Sophie is sharp and playful, and Blake is a dry strategic observer. Each exchange succeeds mechanically, remains active, and provides bespoke continuations alongside exactly one graceful exit. Close-turn memories preserve concrete details from each conversation and consistently use first-person holder perspectives, including correctly attributed NPC-held memories. The relationship outcomes and deltas are deterministic engine results and remain consistent with the successful conversations.

Examples include Chloe's sister's baby and before-thirty anxiety; Jordan's performance-machine metaphor and attention concerns; Maya's runway compliment; Marcus's toaster-reflection roast and knee joke; Liam's Cardiff restoration work and family support; Nia's read that the player chooses words carefully; Sophie's bedroom-system challenge; and Blake's specific read of Sophie. Curator memories retain these same beats for the player, each target NPC, and relevant witnesses.

Acceptance rubric · 3 criteria
  1. cast_voice_separationAcross the complete round, all eight Heartbreakers remain recognisably distinct in temperament, gendered voice, conversational intent, and response rhythm. Fail if several speakers collapse into the same generic flirt or warm-friend voice.
  2. conversation_surfaces_playableEvery chat answers its authored intent specifically and offers concrete continuations for that exact beat plus a graceful exit, without placeholder or duplicate choices.
  3. memories_faithful_across_castClose-turn memories consistently use each holder's perspective, name the other participant, and preserve only beats visible in that Heartbreaker's conversation.
